# Sakuya Cheat Menu Pro - Block 8 Fun Features Scripted GUIs

sakuya_b8_has_marked_country_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b8_has_marked_character_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		OR = {
			any_country = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_variable = sakuya_mark_character_target
				}
			}
			any_character_in_exile_pool = {
				has_variable = sakuya_mark_character_target
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b8_country_set_recognized = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		set_country_type = recognized
	}
}

sakuya_b8_country_set_unrecognized = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		set_country_type = unrecognized
	}
}

sakuya_b8_marked_country_set_recognized = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		sakuya_b3_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_country_found
				exists = scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope
			}
			scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope = {
				set_country_type = recognized
			}
		}
		remove_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_country_found
	}
}

sakuya_b8_marked_country_set_unrecognized = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		sakuya_b3_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_country_found
				exists = scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope
			}
			scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope = {
				set_country_type = unrecognized
			}
		}
		remove_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_country_found
	}
}

sakuya_b8_country_add_regency = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_has_monarchy_law = yes
				has_gov_regency = no
			}
			add_regency_modifier = yes
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b8_country_remove_regency = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_has_monarchy_law = yes
				has_gov_regency = yes
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b8_marked_country_add_regency = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		sakuya_b3_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_country_found
				exists = scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope
				scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope = {
					country_has_monarchy_law = yes
					has_gov_regency = no
				}
			}
			scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope = {
				add_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		remove_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_country_found
	}
}

sakuya_b8_marked_country_remove_regency = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		sakuya_b3_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_country_found
				exists = scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope
				scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope = {
					country_has_monarchy_law = yes
					has_gov_regency = yes
				}
			}
			scope:sakuya_b3_marked_country_scope = {
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		remove_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_country_found
	}
}

	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {

sakuya_b8_marked_character_retire = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		sakuya_b3_resolve_marked_character_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_character_found
				exists = scope:sakuya_b3_marked_character_scope
			}
			scope:sakuya_b3_marked_character_scope = {
				retire_character = yes
			}
		}
		remove_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_character_found
	}
}

sakuya_b8_marked_character_martyr_exile = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		sakuya_b3_resolve_marked_character_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_character_found
				exists = scope:sakuya_b3_marked_character_scope
			}
			scope:sakuya_b3_marked_character_scope = {
				exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
			}
		}
		remove_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_character_found
	}
}

sakuya_b8_marked_character_disinherit = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		sakuya_b3_resolve_marked_character_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_character_found
				exists = scope:sakuya_b3_marked_character_scope
			}
			scope:sakuya_b3_marked_character_scope = {
				disinherit_character = yes
			}
		}
		remove_variable = sakuya_b3_marked_character_found
	}
}

	scope = country

		}

}

sakuya_b8_summon_marx_ruler = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		create_character = {
			template = sakuya_famous_marx_ruler
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b8_tab1_tech_research_speed_add = { scope = country is_shown = { is_player = yes } effect = { sakuya_b8_tab1_change_country_modifier_delta_effect = { MOD = sakuya_cheat_b8_tab1_tech_research_speed VAR = sakuya_b8_tab1_tech_research_speed_mult SMALL_VAR = sakuya_b8_tab1_tech_research_speed_small_mult ACTIVE = sakuya_b8_tab1_tech_research_speed_active DELTA = 0.2 } } }

sakuya_b8_culture_has_selected_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		has_variable = sakuya_list_confirmed_culture
	}
}

sakuya_b8_culture_player_marked_state_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		has_variable = sakuya_list_confirmed_culture
		any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
	}
}

sakuya_b8_culture_target_marked_state_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		has_variable = sakuya_list_confirmed_culture
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target
			any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
		}
	}
}
